### [150] [Meta-Adaptive Beam Search Planning for Transformer-Based Reinforcement Learning Control of UAVs with Overhead Manipulators under Flight Disturbances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26612)
*Hazim Alzorgan,Sayed Pedram Haeri Boroujeni,Abolfazl Razi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于Transformer DDQN的强化学习框架，通过自适应波束搜索规划器解决无人机机械臂耦合运动中的轨迹跟踪问题，显著提升稳定性和精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机搭载机械臂时，机体运动与机械臂运动紧密耦合，即使微小的姿态变化（如风扰或控制误差）也会导致末端执行器偏离预定轨迹。这种耦合使得可靠跟踪变得困难，也限制了原本为固定基座机器人设计的学习型控制器在无人机上的直接应用。

Method: 开发基于强化学习的框架，采用Transformer双深度Q网络（DDQN），核心是自适应波束搜索规划器：通过短时域波束搜索候选控制序列，使用学习到的critic作为前向估计器。该方法采用软件在环（SITL）方式，通过模拟推演而非直接在真实模型上执行动作来预测末端执行器运动。Transformer critic处理状态短序列提供价值估计，DDQN骨干网络提供保持学习稳定所需的一步目标。

Result: 在相同训练条件下评估3自由度空中机械臂，提出的元自适应规划器表现出最强整体性能：奖励提升10.2%，平均跟踪误差显著降低（从约6%降至3%），综合奖励-误差指标相对DDQN基线提升29.6%。在无人机基座受外部扰动漂移时，能保持5厘米跟踪误差，表现出更高的轨迹跟踪稳定性。

Conclusion: 提出的基于Transformer DDQN的自适应波束搜索规划器能有效解决无人机机械臂耦合运动问题，显著提升末端执行器轨迹跟踪的稳定性和精度，特别是在存在外部扰动的情况下。

Abstract: Drones equipped with overhead manipulators offer unique capabilities for inspection, maintenance, and contact-based interaction. However, the motion of the drone and its manipulator is tightly linked, and even small attitude changes caused by wind or control imperfections shift the end-effector away from its intended path. This coupling makes reliable tracking difficult and also limits the direct use of learning-based arm controllers that were originally designed for fixed-base robots. These effects appear consistently in our tests whenever the UAV body experiences drift or rapid attitude corrections. To address this behavior, we develop a reinforcement-learning (RL) framework with a transformer-based double deep Q learning (DDQN), with the core idea of using an adaptive beam-search planner that applies a short-horizon beam search over candidate control sequences using the learned critic as the forward estimator. This allows the controller to anticipate the end-effector's motion through simulated rollouts rather than executing those actions directly on the actual model, realizing a software-in-the-loop (SITL) approach. The lookahead relies on value estimates from a Transformer critic that processes short sequences of states, while a DDQN backbone provides the one-step targets needed to keep the learning process stable. Evaluated on a 3-DoF aerial manipulator under identical training conditions, the proposed meta-adaptive planner shows the strongest overall performance with a 10.2% reward increase, a substantial reduction in mean tracking error (from about 6% to 3%), and a 29.6% improvement in the combined reward-error metric relative to the DDQN baseline. Our method exhibits elevated stability in tracking target tip trajectory (by maintaining 5 cm tracking error) when the drone base exhibits drifts due to external disturbances, as opposed to the fixed-beam and Transformer-only variants.

</details>


### [151] [Partial Motion Imitation for Learning Cart Pushing with Legged Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26659)
*Mili Das,Morgan Byrd,Donghoon Baek,Sehoon Ha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种部分模仿学习方法，将四足机器人的稳定步态迁移到推车操作任务中，通过仅模仿下半身动作实现鲁棒的移动操作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作是四足机器人执行实际任务的关键能力，但学习鲁棒的移动操作技能具有挑战性，因为需要在保持稳定步态的同时执行精确操作

Method: 采用部分模仿学习方法：1) 首先通过大量领域和地形随机化训练鲁棒的步态策略；2) 然后使用部分对抗运动先验，仅模仿下半身动作来学习移动操作策略

Result: 学习的策略在IsaacLab中成功沿不同轨迹推车，并能有效迁移到MuJoCo。与多个基线方法相比，该方法实现了更稳定和准确的移动操作行为

Conclusion: 部分模仿学习方法能够有效将鲁棒步态迁移到移动操作任务中，为四足机器人在现实环境中执行移动操作任务提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Loco-manipulation is a key capability for legged robots to perform practical mobile manipulation tasks, such as transporting and pushing objects, in real-world environments. However, learning robust loco-manipulation skills remains challenging due to the difficulty of maintaining stable locomotion while simultaneously performing precise manipulation behaviors. This work proposes a partial imitation learning approach that transfers the locomotion style learned from a locomotion task to cart loco-manipulation. A robust locomotion policy is first trained with extensive domain and terrain randomization, and a loco-manipulation policy is then learned by imitating only lower-body motions using a partial adversarial motion prior. We conduct experiments demonstrating that the learned policy successfully pushes a cart along diverse trajectories in IsaacLab and transfers effectively to MuJoCo. We also compare our method to several baselines and show that the proposed approach achieves more stable and accurate loco-manipulation behaviors.

</details>


### [152] [Ruka-v2: Tendon Driven Open-Source Dexterous Hand with Wrist and Abduction for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26660)
*Xinqi,Liu,Ruoxi Hu,Alejandro Ojeda Olarte,Zhuoran Chen,Kenny Ma,Charles Cheng Ji,Lerrel Pinto,Raunaq Bhirangi,Irmak Guzey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Ruka-v2是Ruka的升级版，增加了手腕2自由度和平行指关节，提升灵巧性，成本低于1300美元，完全开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ruka虽然开源且成本低，但缺乏手腕活动度和手指内收/外展自由度，限制了模仿人类行为的能力。

Method: 设计带有解耦2自由度平行手腕和手指内收/外展功能的肌腱驱动人形手，保持开源和低成本特性。

Result: 用户研究表明，相比Ruka，任务完成时间减少51.3%，成功率提高21.2%；展示了13个灵巧任务的遥操作和3个任务的自主策略学习。

Conclusion: Ruka-v2通过增加关键自由度显著提升了灵巧性和实用性，为机器人学习提供了可访问的硬件平台，所有设计文件开源。

Abstract: Lack of accessible and dexterous robot hardware has been a significant bottleneck to achieving human-level dexterity in robots. Last year, we released Ruka, a fully open-sourced, tendon-driven humanoid hand with 11 degrees of freedom - 2 per finger and 3 at the thumb - buildable for under $1,300. It was one of the first fully open-sourced humanoid hands, and introduced a novel data-driven approach to finger control that captures tendon dynamics within the control system. Despite these contributions, Ruka lacked two degrees of freedom essential for closely imitating human behavior: wrist mobility and finger adduction/abduction. In this paper, we introduce Ruka-v2: a fully open-sourced, tendon-driven humanoid hand featuring a decoupled 2-DOF parallel wrist and abduction/adduction at the fingers. The parallel wrist adds smooth, independent flexion/extension and radial/ulnar deviation, enabling manipulation in confined environments such as cabinets. Abduction enables motions such as grasping thin objects, in-hand rotation, and calligraphy. We present the design of Ruka-v2 and evaluate it against Ruka through user studies on teleoperated tasks, finding a 51.3% reduction in completion time and a 21.2% increase in success rate. We further demonstrate its full range of applications for robot learning: bimanual and single-arm teleoperation across 13 dexterous tasks, and autonomous policy learning on 3 tasks. All 3D print files, assembly instructions, controller software, and videos are available at https://ruka-hand-v2.github.io/ .

</details>


### [153] [VLA-OPD: Bridging Offline SFT and Online RL for Vision-Language-Action Models via On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26666)
*Zhide Zhong,Haodong Yan,Junfeng Li,Junjie He,Tianran Zhang,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA-OPD：一种结合SFT效率和RL鲁棒性的在线策略蒸馏框架，通过专家教师提供密集的token级监督，使用Reverse-KL目标避免熵爆炸或崩溃，在机器人操作任务中显著提升样本效率和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型后训练方法存在局限性：离线监督微调（SFT）受分布偏移和灾难性遗忘影响，而在线强化学习（RL）则面临稀疏奖励和样本效率低的问题。需要一种既能保持预训练能力又能高效学习新技能的方法。

Method: 提出VLA-OPD框架，利用专家教师对学生自生成轨迹提供密集的token级监督，实现主动错误纠正。采用Reverse-KL目标而非传统的Forward-KL或Hard-CE，避免熵爆炸或过早熵崩溃，通过有界的模式寻求目标过滤教师认知不确定性，同时保持动作多样性。

Result: 在LIBERO和RoboTwin2.0基准测试中，VLA-OPD相比RL显著提升样本效率，相比SFT提高鲁棒性，同时有效缓解后训练过程中的灾难性遗忘问题。

Conclusion: VLA-OPD成功桥接了SFT效率和RL鲁棒性，通过专家教师的密集监督和Reverse-KL目标实现了稳定策略学习，为VLA模型后训练提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Although pre-trained Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models exhibit impressive generalization in robotic manipulation, post-training remains crucial to ensure reliable performance during deployment. However, standard offline Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) suffers from distribution shifts and catastrophic forgetting of pre-trained capabilities, while online Reinforcement Learning (RL) struggles with sparse rewards and poor sample efficiency. In this paper, we propose On-Policy VLA Distillation (VLA-OPD), a framework bridging the efficiency of SFT with the robustness of RL. Instead of relying on sparse environmental rewards, VLA-OPD leverages an expert teacher to provide dense, token-level supervision on the student's self-generated trajectories. This enables active error correction on policy-induced states while preserving pre-trained general capabilities through gentle alignment. Crucially, we formulate VLA-OPD via a Reverse-KL objective. Unlike standard Forward-KL that induces mode-covering entropy explosion, or Hard-CE that causes premature entropy collapse, our bounded mode-seeking objective ensures stable policy learning by filtering out the teacher's epistemic uncertainty while maintaining action diversity. Experiments on LIBERO and RoboTwin2.0 benchmarks demonstrate that VLA-OPD significantly improves sample efficiency over RL and robustness over SFT, while effectively mitigating catastrophic forgetting during post-training.

</details>
